## Console commands

Public (`src/BigDeckOptimization/Commands`, in every build):

- `bdopt [on|off]` — every optimization on/off at runtime (vanilla when off), or the state of each one (incl. patches skipped or self-disabled). Local. Each option gives the same results on or off, so peers may differ, with one exception: the whole mod off during a co-op run also turns off the fast combat start the other players keep, so this player starts each combat far later and the game can drop the actions reaching a player still starting a combat (desync or a stuck turn, "Co-op with a player without the mod"). In a co-op game `bdopt off` prints a warning and needs `bdopt off yes`, and since the re-audit `bdopt on` needs `bdopt on yes` too (a player turning it on in an all-off game makes the same mixed pair); the menu's master switch can't be switched either way during a co-op run; `bdopt` with the mod off in a co-op game says every player must have the same state. "Co-op game" (`Settings.InCoopGame`) is a run (`RunManager.IsInProgress`, not singleplayer), the run manager's net session connected as host or client (`NetSessionConnected`: a mod reloading a co-op save or returning to a lobby mid-run keeps it connected while no run or lobby object exists), or a connected co-op lobby: a scene-tree walk, only when the whole mod is switched or `bdopt` shows the state while it is off (never on a hot path; a node type or field it can't read is skipped, the rest of the tree still walked), for a `StartRunLobby` (character select, custom, daily, v0.113's other modes) or `LoadRunLobby` (a co-op save's load lobby) field whose net service is connected as host or client. The menu checks the run only (BaseLib's mod configuration can't be opened from a lobby), `bdperf opt on|off` the run only. Under `--fastmp` (the test tooling's co-op) the lobby already exists when a script's first line runs: a mod-off control is launched off instead (`launchoff`, `--bdoff`). Not when the mod was turned off at the start (nothing of it runs this session). Joining is guarded too: with the whole mod off, `CoopModeTag` makes the game refuse a player whose on/off state differs from the host's.
- `bdopt <option> on|off` — one optimization: any option key, `bdopt` with no argument lists them (20: `instant_combats`, `fast_combat_start`, `coop_checksum`, `combat_refresh`, `hook_filter`, `relic_input`, `relic_flashes`, `card_flights`, `gc_pacing`, `grid_sort`, `grid_pool`, `pile_lookup`, `card_ids`, `pile_counters`, `listeners`, `hook_skip`, `hover_tips`, `limitless_tabs`, `limitless_saves`, `card_type_line`); `bdopt fast_combat_start_relics <0-100>` sets the fast combat start's relic threshold (BaseLib slider). Saved in BaseLib's mod configuration. Never re-enables a patch that turned itself off after an error.
- `bdopt why` — every part of an optimization another mod keeps on the normal game, grouped by option: `Patch: Type.Method <- Mod1, Mod2` for a back-off from a method another mod patches, a sentence for a note (another mod's types or saved data), then a count per mod. `bdopt` ends with a line pointing to it when the mod is on and a part that runs has a back-off (with the mod off, `bdopt why` still lists every recorded one). `bdopt why` ends with the optimizations inactive now and those partly inactive (the menu's red and orange titles, `Settings.StateOf`), and marks a back-off that keeps all of its part vanilla "(all of that part)". Both check first (`Settings.RefreshConflicts`, answers younger than 2 s reused).
- `bdopt` is not a debug-only command (`DebugOnly => false`): since game v0.113 a debug command marks the run as having used one in its save (`ExtraFields.MarkDebugToolUsed`), even to show the state.

## Test tooling (`src/BigDeckOptimization/Bench`, Debug builds only: excluded from Release by the csproj, `BENCH` symbol)

- `bigdeck <count> [card-id] [seed]` — fill the deck up to count (one card, or random from the character pool). Networked + seeded (seed 0 default): usable in co-op if **every** player has the mod.
- `bigdeck relics <count> [relic-id] [seed]` — add relics (character + shared pools), no obtain effects. Networked.
- `bigdeck import <file.run|shared-name> [all|i|i,j] [cards|relics|all] [hash]` — exact decks (upgrades, enchantments) and relics from a run history file, added like a save load (no obtain/card-added hooks). Players can be merged. In co-op: only bare names from the shared folder `user://bigdeck` (`%APPDATA%\SlayTheSpire2\bigdeck`), and the SHA-256 prefix from `bigdeck hash <name>` is required: the peers that import provably read the same bytes, but a peer whose file is missing, unreadable (a locked file is a refusal, not an exception in the action queue) or different refuses with an error while the others import, and the run diverges: run `bigdeck hash` on every machine first. Barrier marks (`bigdeck mark`) are cleared at the first mark made in another run.
- `bigdeck tank [hp]` — sets every living enemy's max and current HP (default 1,000,000) for long perf-test combats. Networked.
- `bigdeck hash <shared-name>` — prints the SHA-256 prefix of a shared run file (each peer prints its own).
- Caps: deck ≤ 5000, ≤ 1000 relics per command (any peer can issue networked commands).
- `bdperf checksum [n]` — checksum cost, rows vanilla (the whole mod off), others, cache, cache+filter, +card reuse; checks identical checksums. Every bench forcing a part on puts its switch back only if the part didn't fault meanwhile, refuses a part that faulted earlier in the session (`faulted earlier in the session ... nothing to compare`), and restores the effect counters afterwards (`Bench/EffectCounters`: the `EFFECT` lines count only the game's own calls).
- `bdperf refresh [n]` — vanilla vs cached post-action refresh (enchantments + hand), checks the game state is identical afterwards; `not applied` when ListenerCache isn't, `not compared` when nothing of this mod served on the cached side.
- `bdperf verify [off|sample|all]` — live verification mode and counters (snapshots, verified, mismatches), patch on/off state. `all` costs a vanilla snapshot per action: singleplayer debugging only.
- `bdperf grid [cards]` — deck view sort orders (vanilla vs cached, identical orders), enchantment tab rendering on cards spread over the deck (vanilla vs reuse, identical tab state), and freeing grid holders and their card nodes to the pool (vanilla vs scan, identical remaining connections, a row each; everything freed before the bench returns). Bench timings of rendering/freeing are far below in-game ones (hidden container, no GC pressure): use them for identity, traces for gains. UI only, usable anywhere in a run.
- `bdperf combatstart` — wall-clock breakdown of the last combat start (phases, GC pauses, longest frames). `checksum` and `refresh` refuse to run in real co-op.
- `bdperf opt on|off` — all optimizations on/off at runtime for an A/B comparison in the same session; never saved (BaseLib saves the player's choice, `Settings.MasterChoice`, at a settings change and on quit; the menu box keeps showing it). In a co-op run `bdperf opt on|off` warns like `bdopt` and needs `bdperf opt on|off yes`, either way (not after a start with the mod off).
- `bdperf costwrites` — makes the first hand card free this turn, on this peer only (a state change): singleplayer only, refused in co-op like `checksum` and `refresh`.
- `bdperf probe [reset|log|on|off|nodes|hitch <ms>]` — `PerfProbe`: per-method calls/total/max (async: also wall time to completion) for about 50 deck-size-sensitive methods, and every frame over the threshold (100 ms) logged with the probes that ran in it.
- `bdperf pile [verify <n>]` — `CardModel.Pile` for every card: index vs vanilla answers and timings; `verify 1` checks every live answer against vanilla.
- `bdperf plaque [n]` — BaseLib's card type line for the first n cards of the local piles, TypePlaqueSkip off then on: same `LocString` object for every card, and the times.
- `bdperf sortkeys [wrong]` — in combat, the local draw, discard and hand cards sorted with the game's comparison (whole mod off) and with CardSortKeys, then `StableShuffle` both ways from one seed: same order, and the times; `wrong` reverses its answers (positive control). On v0.113+, then the same sort with a Veilpiercer on the local player (put on and taken off again with the game's internal calls, silently, before the bench returns; its icon shows and goes): CardSortKeys must answer, not refuse (a refusal for a keyword listener of the combat not known to only add keywords, an unknown override or a known adder another mod patches, is `not compared`: told by `CardSortKeys.LastRefusalKeywordListener`, not by the refusal's wording), with the game's order.
- `bdperf fault piles|walk` — the next filtered pile walk throws inside FastListeners' cache, or the next filtered mirror walk throws after its verdicts: tests of FastListeners' two fallbacks. `bdperf snapgameerror <n>` — the next n card reads of SnapshotCardReuse throw as the game's own code would: the piles go to the game's `From`, the part stays on (`GameExceptions`).
- `bdperf reserialize [verify <n>]`, `bdperf clonememo [verify <n>]` — LimitlessReserialize's kept/rebuilt/verified/mismatch counters (one kept list in n compared with a rebuilt one), LimitlessCloneMemo's reused/verified/options reused; a report after `verify` that compared nothing fails.
- Local co-op (`tools/autotest/coop.ps1`, see its README): two instances launched directly with `--force-steam off` (no Steam, so only the local `mods` folder loads: vanilla + this mod; each instance has its own data folder `user://default/<net id>`, the first launch needs the mods warning accepted once per folder), `--fastmp host_standard` / `--fastmp join --clientId 1001` (ENet on 127.0.0.1: 33771 for `coop.ps1`, 34000 + test slot for `parcoop.ps1` and `coop4.ps1`, so two series never share a port), `--log-file <path>` for separate logs, and `--bdtest <script>`. Script `mplobby <CHARACTER> [players] [seed]` picks, sets the run seed (default `BDTEST`) and readies up; console commands go through the action queue in co-op. The test runner fixes the `fight` command's encounter seed to 12345 (the game's is the clock in seconds up to v0.111, `Rng.CreateChaoticSeed` from v0.113: else peers desync) and answers card choices identically on both peers (deterministic selector, which also bypasses choice syncing). Imports of runs played with mods cap upgrade levels / skip unknown cards and relics. Fur Coat throws on `fight` before visiting the map (`CurrentMapPoint` null) and the combat never starts: the runner skips it when there is no map point. `-WithMods` copies the test mods into `<game>/mods_STEAMTEST` (read without Steam: parlib.ps1's `Get-TestModIds`, the supported mods BaseLib, Limitless and BetterSpire2, plus `BDTEST_EXTRA_MODS` and the account's enabled Workshop mods; `BDTEST_MODS` replaces the list, `none` leaves only this mod) and removes it when the last series using it on that copy ends (parlib.ps1 `Remove-TestMods`; a crashed series' holder is stale and does not keep it): a normal launch reads it too.
- `AutoTest` — unattended sessions (launchers and scenarios in `tools/autotest`): on a game copy (a `steam_appid.txt` next to its exe, `-GameDir`/`STS2_GAME_DIR`) the exe is launched directly with `--force-steam off --bdtest <script>`, in a test slot's own user data with default settings, and the test mods (BaseLib, Limitless, BetterSpire2, the account's enabled mods, `BDTEST_EXTRA_MODS`; `BDTEST_MODS` replaces the list) copied into the copy's `mods_STEAMTEST` for the series; each one must log its initialization. Through Steam (`-applaunch 2868840`) only on the Steam install, which `run.ps1` refuses without a Debug build there; the tests no longer use it. A session whose runner logs nothing in 90 s is closed. Uses save profile 3 in memory only (the last-profile setting is untouched), disables tutorials there, answers card choices with the first options, quits at the end/on timeout. Script lines: `newrun <CHARACTER> [seed]`, `wait <s>`, `waitfor menu|run|combat|idle|nocombat [timeout]`, `cmd <console command>`, `play <n>` (up to n), `endturn` (waits until the game took the end of turn), `enemyhp <hp>` (in co-op: before this peer's combat is set up, else the line fails), `state [label] [optional]`, `playcard <ID>` (after `cmd card <ID> hand`), `handselect <ID> [hold <s>] [free]` (a hand choice held open, then ended by the game's revalidation), `speed normal|fast|instant` (sessions start in Fast), `marker`, `log`, `timeout <s>`, `quit`; `restsite <OptionClass>` and `eventoption <text key part>` pick a rest site / event option like a click, timed; `waitfor deckcount|restsite|event` (deck counter caught up, rest site options shown, event running); `nodes [label]` (scene node count, card previews, flying cards), `watch <s>` (previews sampled 4×/s in the background), `counters [label]` (cache/patch counters); `pilescroll draw|discard|exhaust <frames> [px]` (a combat pile view scrolled like a held mouse wheel), `language <code>` / `menuback` / `menutext <SLUG>` (menu texts per game language), `fakeconflict <Type:Method>` (simulates another mod patching a method). Sessions launched by `run.ps1`/`coop.ps1` run off-screen and muted (`--bdhidden`, `--bdmute`; `-Visible`/`-Sound` to opt out). `--bdshow <screen>` (fullscreen and on top on that screen, mute-in-background skipped), `deckfilter`; `newrun` stops the menu music as the character select screen does (it kept playing under the act music). `bdperf checks on|off` turns the Debug-only vanilla re-checks off to measure release-like timings. For dotnet-trace, keep the game alive until the trace ends (symbols are only written at the end of a trace on a live process).
- Vanilla mod-off sessions (`--bdvanilla`, 2026-11-16): a solo mod-off scenario (named off, `cmd bdopt off` before its run) is launched with `--bdvanilla` (`parlib.ps1`, so `par.ps1` and `parsweep.ps1`): `ModEntry.StartedOff` from the start, as a player's saved off, so no optimization patch is installed, only the test tooling and `CoopModeTag`; session only, never saved, `Rewritten` untouched; logged once (`BDVANILLA: launched with --bdvanilla`, the only key `judge.py` reads to accept the mod off from the start, and `parlib.ps1` requires it of a vanilla job and refuses it in any other). Before, the mod-off side ran with every patch installed then `bdopt off`: its log depended on the patches' code, so the rule was a full mod-off run without baselines once per release and version (mod off is ~85% of a sweep's time), and a disabled patch still changing the game showed on both sides of an A/B. Now a baseline depends on no patch (`Get-BaselineKey`: game, test mods and their data, run files, profile, `mod_configs`/`mod_data`, the `Bench` parts the scenario runs, the scenario; `ref=vanilla1` keeps the older patched-off logs out) and `-UseBaselines` is allowed for the release run. The proof that replaces the rule: `vanillacheck.ps1` on every supported version each release, the same mod-off scenarios vanilla and patched-off (`-PatchedOff`), `IDENTICAL` (the project rule "`bdopt off` must be the unmodified game", checked directly for the first time). Co-op mod-off sides (`parcoop.ps1`, `coop.ps1`, `coop4.ps1`, `--bdoff`) stay patched-off for now.

- Other game versions without switching Steam's branch (2026-10-26): full copies `C:\Games\StS2-public-0.111.1` and `C:\Games\StS2-beta-0.113.1` (the two tested since 2026-11-17; `-0.107.1`, `-0.111.0` and `-0.113.0` kept, no longer distributed nor tested) with a `steam_appid.txt` (2868840) next to the exe, launched directly (first with Steam initializing and the Workshop mods loading; the test runners now launch them without Steam, above): `run.ps1 -GameDir <copy>`, `coop.ps1` with `$env:STS2_GAME_DIR`, deploy with `-p:ModDeployDir=<copy>\mods\BigDeckOptimization`. Builds target v0.107.1's assemblies (`refs/v0.107.1`, `Directory.Build.local.props`), the oldest version the DLL has code paths for, whatever version is public: the test tooling names a few of its members at compile time (PerfProbe's `nameof` of `CombatManager.EndCombatInternal` and the turn phase methods, public in v0.107.1 only), and `tools/compat` checks the DLL against every other version. `tools/compat` lists every member of the DLL a version lacks; `tools/fpdiff review` every fingerprinted method a version changed (diffs, new callees, renames; `hookfps` feeds `tools/hookskip/classify.py`, `describe` prints a method's fingerprinted code); `tools/mutate` writes modified game/mod assemblies for `tools/autotest/mutations.ps1`. `tools/check_versions.py` also asks Steam what is published (builds of the public and public-beta branches through SteamCMD's anonymous login, the Workshop update times of every watched mod installed (BaseLib, Limitless, BetterSpire2, SpeedX, Unlimited) through the Steam Web API), so an update players get but this machine hasn't downloaded yet shows up.
- Scenarios added for v0.6.1–v0.6.4: `guard-checks` (`faultprobe`), `started-off`, `pace-on/off/vanilla/instant` (enemy turn times vs vanilla), `menu-instant`, `coopinstant` (one peer with instant combats), `ab-2k-off/on` (all optimizations A/B at ~1,900 cards: short enough for a vanilla run on the beta), `coopbig` (co-op timings with ~3,700 cards each), `cooptry` (hands-on co-op: `coop.ps1 -HostVisible`, you play the host, the scripted fake player uses `selector local` so it never answers your card choices). Run `unlock all` once on a new test profile (the non-Steam co-op profiles `default/1` and `default/1001` are unlocked, and set to English, since 2026-10-27).

- Test coverage hardening (2026-11-17, branch `hardening-coverage`, from the three test audits; DESIGN's patches unchanged but the CardDbScan fix below, every hook test-only under `BENCH`):
  - Script `checksum <label> vanilla` fails unless the immediately preceding checksum line was the plain `checksum <label>` with the same value; `checksum <label> same` likewise against the preceding line of that label (snapcheckcoop-host after `snapmoved off`). A `CHECKSUMPAIR <label> vanilla|same: A vs B identical|DIFFERENT` line says what was compared. Before, nothing compared the two lines of one session (a vanilla value set to anything passed).
  - After every checksum line: `STATE <label>: cardids <hash> (<n> ids, next <id>, <m> without)`, the network ids `NetCombatCardDb` gave the cards of every player's combat piles (players, piles in `AllPiles` order, cards in pile order); `compare.py` compares it as any STATE line. The checksum covers no card id, though co-op actions name cards by them: CardDbScan's result was invisible to every A/B.
  - The ABORT line ends with `[lines left N]` (script lines not run: neither blank nor `#`), and the script's `expect` lines are logged at its start as `EXPECTDECL <regex>` (judge.py: an ABORT after an expected failure waives the end only at N = 0; an expect an ABORT left unrun is still known).
  - `effectcheck all ran [except <regex>]` (every effect of the session above zero; the regex is searched in the name and in `<name>@<game version>`: `ReplayStateSkip@v0\.107\.`), `effectcheck <Effect> ran` (above zero now, no earlier label needed); every effectcheck says `skipped: the mod is off` in a mod-off session (an off twin keeps the on side's steps). Effects-check ends with `effectcheck all ran` (after `onfail continue`, so a mutation losing an effect on purpose still reaches quit); effects-coop and release-checks name what they are there to make run.
  - Opt-in effects, listed only where a script names them in an `effectcheck` line (listed everywhere they would read as lost in sessions that can't run them): `SnapshotVerifier` (re-checks), `ListenerCache.Reused`, `FastListeners.ModifierSkip`, `HookSkip.BaseLib` (BaseLib's interface hooks' filtered walks), `ExtraScopes.BetterSpire2`, `CardDbScan.Verified`, `HookFilter.<Mirror>` per mirror (`Damage`, `DamageLate`: v0.113's PrefixWithLate, `EnergyCost`, `Keywords`, `ShouldPlay`, `Hitting`, `EnergyCostColor`, `StarCostColor`). Listed once a Dread or Blitz run started: `FastListeners.Dominion`, `FastListeners.ModeRunModels` (the walks' v0.113 tails meeting a dominion, run models beyond the scaling one), `ListenerCache.Dominion`, `ListenerCache.ModeRunModels` (their setters' invalidations). Every one is put back after a bench (`EffectCounters`).
  - Game modes: `newrun <CHARACTER> <seed> dread|blitz`, `autoslay <seed> [state] dread|blitz` (v0.113+); `dominion [require]` logs `STATE dominion: run X, combat Y, run models [...]` and fails without a combat dominion with `require`. `requiregame v<x.y>`. A mode or version the game lacks logs `SKIPPED: <why> ... [lines left N]` and ends the script as finished (both sides of an A/B alike).
  - Positive controls of the safety nets (`Patches/TestWrong.cs`): `bdperf <part> wrong [variant]` arms one wrong answer the net's next check sees, the check run at once: `segments` (VerifySegment), `segments wrong moves|subscribers|shifts` (CheckMoveHit, CheckSubscribers, MatchesPiles), `reuse wrong trusted|moved|card` (SnapshotCardReuse's trusted pile full read, moved pile check, card serialization compare), `pilebits`, `pile` (PileIndex's sampled scan), `clonememo`, `reserialize`, `recalc`, `hookskip`, `listenercache` (CheckedAgainstKept), `hookfilter wrong walk|hitting|cost` (bench checks, `bdperf checks on`). `bdperf wrong [check]` lists what is armed (`check` fails while one is). Scenarios `wrong-<net>-1k-on`, each against `wrong-1k-off` (`# compare-with:`): SnapshotVerifier off, the detection expected, the injection taken.
  - CardDbScan: `bdperf cardids [blind|failadd]` (blind: the next add of a card without an ID left unrecorded and every skipped walk checked: the walk right after it must see it and go vanilla, IDing the card where the game does; failadd: an add the game refuses, a canonical card, must leave no card pending), `bdperf pendingcap [N]` (the per-pile pending limit, 256, lowered; a report after a set limit fails without an overflow). Scenarios cardblind-1k-on/off, pendingcap-1k-on/off.
  - Bug fixed (performance only, Release too): RecordAdd records a card before `AddInternal` runs; an add whose original threw without putting the card in the pile left it pending, no ID ever given, so that pile walked vanilla until combat end or 256 more adds. AfterAdd drops it again (`DroppedFailedAdds`, `bdperf cardids failadd`).
  - Back-offs: `fakeconflict` takes any loaded mod's method (`Type+Nested:Method`, `Method#n`, `Method(ParamType)`, `Prefix*` for a compiler-named lambda); ForeignPatches shapes `skip-prefix` and `skip-and-add-prefix` (Squeeze-gated bool prefixes on `CardPile.AddInternal`/`RemoveInternal`, the second writing the pile's list itself: PileIndex's and CardDbScan's `!__runOriginal` branches, counted in COUNTERS). `bdperf baselibtypes assume <type>|clear`: BaseLibTypes answers as if a mod defined such a type (the other side of FastListeners' card modifier skip; this DLL can't reference BaseLib and a dynamic assembly isn't looked at). Scenarios audit-conflict-limitless-2k-on, audit-conflict-display-1k-on, sortkeys-conflict-1k-on, foreign-skip-1k-on/off, foreign-skipadd-1k-on/off.
  - Faults and co-op: guard-walk-on (`bdperf fault walk`, vs guard-async-ref), snapgameerror-1k-on, reserialize-verify-1k-on, audit-coopdeath-host checking walks, listeners, snapshots and checksums after the death, coopsample-host/client (450 cards each: SnapshotVerifier's Sample mode, skipped over 1,000 combat cards, runs in co-op), audit-verifier-relics (the SnapshotCardReuse effects stop after the verifier's mismatch), fast-start-check (no wait skipped after the combat start), fast-start-relics (the solo relic threshold), effects-fine-1k-on (the opt-in effects), dread-1k-on/off, blitz-1k-on/off.
  - Save and continue in a new process: `tools/autotest/continue-check.ps1` (kept slot): continue-save builds and saves a run, continue-load-on continues it in a fresh game (lists from the save's JSON, notes from saved pairs, empty memos: effect checks), the save is put back, continue-load-off continues it with the mod off from its launch; the two load logs must compare IDENTICAL.
  - Mutations (tools/mutate): `execfinished-add`, `execfinished-clone`, `hand-revalidate`, `hand-select`, `orb-enqueue`.

## Distribution

- Steam Workshop (primary), GitHub (MIT, private until v0.1) for source and releases, Nexus on demand.
- ID `BigDeckOptimization`, display name "Big Deck Optimization".
- README / Workshop text: don't present Limitless as the source of big decks; only list it among compatible mods.

### Lesson (stress trace 2, 2026-10-21)

Per-handler scopes cost more than they saved: each outermost scope rebuilt and re-filtered ~12k listeners for one or two hook calls (filter 7.3 s + rebuild 2.8 s per 60 s). Scopes must be coarse (one per refresh) and the filter single-pass.

### Stress results (2026-10-21, singleplayer, ~4,690 cards + ~1,840 relics = 5x the reference run)

| Build | Refreshes / 60 s | Avg per refresh | `bdperf refresh` |
|---|---|---|---|
| cache only | 670 | 27.4 ms | — |
| + HookFilter, per-handler scopes | 625 | 29.8 ms | x5.9 (hand only) |
| + single dispatch scope, single-pass filter | 798 | 16.9 ms | 152 → 14 ms, x10.7 |

Remaining per refresh (~12 ms inside the scope): run listener materialization ~2.3 ms, filter ~1.6 ms, Limitless enchantment visuals, CardCostHelper energy/star cost loops. Out of scope: gameplay hooks (damage, HP loss, after-damage...) ~20 ms per hit at this load — caching them needs lazy per-item `Contains` semantics and a dedicated verifier (desync risk), deferred to v0.2. Reusing lists across refreshes was rejected: BaseLib card modifiers can change between refreshes without a hook we can track.
